# REPRODUCE: exact commands, inputs and expected checks

Final version for closure matrix v185 (2026-10-29T19:26Z). Exact commands, inputs and expected checks for the numerical
results and theoretical certificates of combined campaign 1. All paths are relative to the root of the accompanying
artifacts (the folder published beside the joint closure report). Python 3.12 with numpy, scipy, mpmath, sympy and
python-flint. Launcher used by the audit lanes (one thread, low priority):

    OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 nice -n 19 python3 -B <script> <args>

Run from a copy of the artifacts folder so that caches and outputs stay out of the published files. Every expected
value below is quoted from the audit entry named in the row; compare the sha256 of the produced artifact with the one
in that entry. The two audit lanes' per-result folders are included as code, proofs and readmes only
(audit/theory_audit/research_code/ and audit/numerical_audit/research_code/); their large outputs, verification
records and staged copies of peer files are not included, and the theory auditor's staging tool
(audit/theory_audit/tools/stage_reproduction.py) expects the original layout, so run a checker directly from a copy of
its folder with the inputs named in its PROOF.md. Paths marked "(not published)" name working files of the run that are
not part of the artifacts; inputs from Changhe Yang's published case4 and analysis2 artifacts are listed in README.md.
Section 0 below records the source integrity check that was run during the campaign.

## 0. Source integrity

    bash working file verify_sources.sh (not published)

Expected: mismatches=0 for all nine trees (listings recorded 2026-10-28T20:32Z to 20:33Z; last check 2026-10-29T15:47Z, a coordinator working file (not published)).
The closure matrix versions are in working file closure_matrix_history (not published)/ (v185, 19:25Z, sha256
b2820f499b70658b16af634ed9e1c0dfa58c1c9fc647115fdd811f1003eb53b6). Theory registry: theory_v08
(exchange/theory_v08.md, 15:30:58Z, sha256
a35184b753f57216c6b4bc01052317c508fe5439fda65b682b0cecb098be8bc9; theory_v01 to v07 are kept beside it).

## 1. Candidate snapshots

Each snapshot directory candidates/<ID>/ is immutable and carries MANIFEST.json and SHA256SUMS:

    cd candidates/<ID> && sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS

INDEX.md lists the sha256 of each MANIFEST.json. Checked by the coordinator for C1-N1-001 to 004 (21:33Z, 21:56Z, 22:17Z), C1-N1-005 (23:30Z), C1-N1-006
(23:34Z) C1-N1-007 (2026-10-29T00:32Z; MANIFEST.json sha256 da19f6ee...) C1-N1-008 (about 00:51Z; MANIFEST.json sha256
7fbfaed4...) C1-N1-009 (about 01:25Z; MANIFEST.json sha256 fc9895de...) C1-N1-010 (01:43Z; MANIFEST.json sha256 2f1c3fc4...), C1-N1-012 (between 03:05Z and 03:15Z), C1-N1-014 (05:27Z) and
C1-N1-011, C1-N1-013, C1-N1-014 again (05:39Z; MANIFEST.json sha256 264708b6..., 75d28547..., 1ebba4b1...), C1-N1-015 (06:19Z;
MANIFEST.json sha256 e7dc4a09...; exact JSON d7759bba...), C1-N1-016 (06:47Z; MANIFEST.json sha256 64872cff...;
exact JSON a0345b4e...):
`sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS` in each snapshot directory, all OK. Read coefficients as exact rationals from the *_exact.json file: rounding them to binary64
reintroduces a forbidden sector for C1-N1-007 (N4-096).

## 4. Lane reproduction documents

Optimization runs, restart instructions and the lane checks are documented in the lanes themselves (read only):
- reports/NUMERICS_FINAL.md section 8 (restart instructions; final, frozen 18:30:12Z, sha256
  e092a45c0ad96062579c267581a575ff8ae6cc39c07588ea00065c8a687d75c2) and code/numerics_basis/README.md with
  code/numerics_basis/SHA256SUMS (582 files).
- code/theory_closure/README.md (195 hashed artifacts; the H18 checks of THEORY_FINAL).
- code/numerics_certification/README.md (the N3 numerics worker enclosure records).
- audit/numerical_audit/AUDIT_REPRODUCE.md (every N4 command) and audit/theory_audit/AUDIT_REPRODUCE.md (every T4 staging command).
The hashes of these files at the end of the run are in a coordinator working file (not published)/ (19:00Z collection).
